## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can open a task to fix a failing test in one of your repos, and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 깨진 테스트 고치기 같은 코딩 작업부터 웹에서 정보 찾기, 메일이나 일정 확인까지 함께 도와드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 알겠어요. 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요. 좋은 밤 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 좋아요. 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたデータを確実に、順番通りに届ける」、UDPは「届く保証はしないが、速くて軽い」。信頼性を取るか速度と軽さを取るかが一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de integrarlos, siempre que la rama no se haya compartido. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir su historial les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的字节流：收发双方先握手建立连接，丢了会重传，数据按顺序交付；UDP 则不建连、不保证送达和顺序，发出去就不管了，所以更快、开销更小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own unpushed branch before sharing it, so history reads as one clean line. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have built on.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我去做：登录页：换成新的输入框组件
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务只做第一件：把登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ 决定已定：三件事按顺序做，这是第一件。 ⏎ 完成标准：登录表单使用新的输入框组件，原有的登录功能不坏，相关的单元测试或类型检查通过，并把跑的命令和结果报告出来。 ⏎ 不要做：不接短信验证码（第二件），不写端到端测试（第三件）。不要合并、推送、开 PR。改动在自己的分支上提交即可。 ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter ⏎  ⏎ The things asked (the user sees each under their message, each ticking off on its own): ⏎ 1. 先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件 ⏎ In report_to_main, give each one's state in `asks`, in this order, with a short note of what came of it (the answer, "added", "PR opened"). A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件 (working); 2. 接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 补登录页的端到端测试 (working)

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/utils.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test utils
```

如果想只跑某个用例而不是整个文件，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to a database is written down before it's applied to the main data, so the database can recover after a crash by replaying the log. Writing changes sequentially to the log is fast and safe, and it lets the database defer the slower work of updating the actual data files.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
  [partner message] I'd take the Mac mini. Same chip and memory, $400 less, and it's built to sit plugged in at home.

The Air's only real plus is the battery surviving a short power cut. If you want it to travel with you, the Air makes sense. For a home server that stays put, the mini wins.

<div class="k-sum">

<script>
var DEF = [
  {t: "G1", p: 553, s: "二等座余 12 张"},
  {t: "G3", p: 553, s: "二等座余 4 张"},
  {t: "G7", p: 626, s: "二等座"}
];
function el(tag, cls, text) {
  var e = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) e.className = cls;
  if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
  return e;
}
function render(rows) {
  rows = (rows && rows.length) ? rows : DEF;
  rows = rows.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.p - b.p; });
  var ok = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.p < 600; });
  document.getElementById("cnt").textContent = ok.length + " 班";
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = ok.length
    ? "最低 ¥" + ok[0].p
    : "今天没有 600 元以下的";
  if (window.ducter) ducter.summary(ok.length + " 班 600 元以下");
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var row = el("div", "k-row");
    var main = el("div", "k-row-main");
    main.appendChild(el("div", "", r.t));
    main.appendChild(el("div", "k-row-sub", r.s || ""));
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(el("div", "k-row-value", "¥" + r.p + (r.p < 600 ? "" : "（超过 600）")));
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
if (window.ducter) {
  render(ducter.get("trips"));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get("trips")); });
} else {
  render(null);
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trips":[{"t":"G1","p":553,"s":"二等座余 12 张"},{"t":"G3","p":553,"s":"二等座余 4 张"},{"t":"G7","p":626,"s":"二等座"}]}

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: "hawdb nightly writes" is on your shelf. It shows the latest night and the run history, starting with nights 2 and 3. Future reports will add to it.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1627 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">–</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var latestEl = document.getElementById('latest');
  var subEl = document.getElementById('sub');
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');

  function render() {
    var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
    listEl.textContent = '';
    if (!nights.length) {
      latestEl.textContent = '–';
      subEl.textContent = 'No runs yet';
      return;
    }
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    latestEl.textContent = Math.round(last.writes / 1000) + 'k writes/s';
    subEl.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · ' + last.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.night;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · ' + n.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = Math.round(n.writes / 1000) + 'k/s';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(val);
      listEl.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  render();
  ducter.onChange(render);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElementById(id); };

  function fmt(n){
    return '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toLocaleString('zh-CN', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
  }
  function esc(s){
    return String(s).replace( … ;
  }

  $('days').addEventListener('click', function(ev){
    var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    var e = items.filter(function(x){ return x.id === id; })[0];
    if (!e) return;
    ducter.ask('删掉「' + e.item + '」这笔？').then(function(r){
      if (!r) return;
      items = items.filter(function(x){ return x.id !== id; });
      save();
    });
  });

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var date = $('d').value.trim();
    var item = $('i').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('a').value);
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) || !item || !(amount > 0)) return;
    items.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), date: date, item: item, amount: amount });
    $('i').value = '';
    $('a').value = '';
    save();
    ducter.summary(items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(items.reduce(function(s, e){ return s + (Number(e.amount) || 0); }, 0)));
  });

  $('d').value = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  items = ducter.get('expenses') || [];
  ducter.onChange(function(){
    items = ducter.get('expenses') || [];
    render();
  });
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

(function () {

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var rm = ev.target.closest('[data-remove]');
    if (rm) {
      var rid = Number(rm.getAttribute('data-remove'));
      items = items.filter(function (e) { return e.id !== rid; });
      openId = null;
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = Number(row.getAttribute('data-id'));
    openId = openId === id ? null : id;
    render();

})();

<script>
(function(){
  var cat = "交通";
  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElementById(id); };
  function esc(s){ return String(s  …          '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.what || x.trip) + '</div>' +

      : '<p class="k-empty">还没记一笔，在下面添加第一笔。</p>';

  $("cats").onclick = function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest(".k-chip");
    if (!b) return;
    cat = b.getAttribute("data-c");
    Array.prototype.forEach.call($("cats").querySelectorAll(".k-chip"), function(c){
      c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", c === b ? "true" : "false");
    });
  };

  $("save").onclick = function(){
    var amt = Number($("amt").value);
    if (!amt) return;
    var xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
    xs.push({
      trip: $("trip").value.trim(),
      what: $("what").value.trim(),
      day: $("day").value.trim(),
      cat: cat,
      amt: amt
    });
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    $("what").value = "";
    $("amt").value = "";
    draw();
  };

  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(draw);
  draw();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶白色的 3D 笔记本电脑图标，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。它柔和、友好，空白屏幕正好表达"还没有内容"，不会让人觉得是出错了。

有两点要注意：
- 屏幕是空的，图本身不太能说明具体是什么空，最好配一句文字，比如"还没有笔记"。
- 它是立体渲染风格，如果你的界面是扁平的，放进去可能有点突兀。奶白色和深蓝边框跟你界面的主色搭得上的话，效果会好很多。

尺寸上，320×246 的原图在空状态里显示 120 到 160 像素宽比较合适。
```

(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让它再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻，补好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

背景：这是对 TTL 测试修复的追加。现在的假时钟测试已经覆盖了过期前后，请再加一条测试，专门覆盖时间正好等于过期时刻（expiry 那一刻）的行为：确认在恰好到期的时间点，该值已经视为过期（或按现有实现约定的边界行为，并在测试里写清楚），不会因为时钟抖动而时好时坏。

完成标准：新测试用假时钟，不依赖真实计时；在同一分支上提交；跑一遍测试套件，报告通过情况和连续运行的结果。

不做的事：不要合并，不要推送，不要开 PR，不要改动其他无关的测试或 Cache.values() 的逻辑。
